  Blocks nothing here. Amended by OP-01/D156. Routed to OP-01's spec flow by OP-01/D156.

`OP-01/O92` and `O93` were found on 2026-11-04, after the freeze at tag
`observe/OP-01/freeze-1`, by a reading of the record's decisions outside a
review (BK-485), and reopen the record under RFC-0030 C4. `O92` runs as a
reopen and `O93` as a gap; both block a freeze.

- `OP-01/O92`: a reopen of OP-01/D6 against D155. Scenario: 10 bytes remain
  on an Open stream; the caller calls `read(0)`, then `read(10)`. D6's
  Decision: "With r bytes remaining, `read(n)` for n ≥ 0 returns min(n, r)
  bytes", so `read(0)` returns `b''`; the same Decision: bytes returned are
  "a prefix of the content, the whole content once `b''` is returned". Both
  cannot hold for `read(0)` with bytes remaining. D155 settled the same
  question for `read1(0)` and a `readinto` or `readinto1` with an empty
  buffer: "none of these moves the position or the state, and on an Open
  stream a `b''` or 0 they return is not the end"; its Amends line names
  D78, D132 and D134, not D6. To decide with the maintainer: whether
  `read(0)` with bytes remaining is the end, and whether it moves the
  position or the state. Dependent: D6, D18's Open row, D132's Exhausted
  cell, D155. Blocks the spec clause for `read(n)` with n = 0. Decided by
  OP-01/D157.
- `OP-01/O93`: a gap in OP-01/D18. Scenario: a stream over a 10-byte item;
  `read(10)` returns all ten bytes; no read has yet returned `b''`; the
  next `read(10)` meets a failure in D96's Stream read column, or is
  cancelled (E14). D18's rows are "Open (bytes remain)" and "Exhausted (at
  end)", and D132, D148 and D155 set the Exhausted cell's values, but no
  decision says when a stream enters Exhausted: once its last byte is
  returned, or once a read reports the end. If Open, a storage failure or a
  failure raised as is moves the stream to Failed (D16, D51); if Exhausted,
  the read returns `b''` (D132), and D51 names only a caller error from
  Exhausted. A cancellation leads to Closed from either state (D20, D50), so
  what is undecided is whether that read can be cancelled at all rather than
  return `b''`. To decide with the maintainer: when a stream enters
  Exhausted, and so whether that read can fail or be cancelled. Dependent:
  D16, D18, D20, D50, D51, D96, D132. Blocks the spec clause for the
  stream's entry into Exhausted and for a read at the last byte. Decided by
  OP-01/D158.

`OP-01/O94` to `O101` were found on 2026-11-04 by `/observe-review OP-01`
after the reopen that decided `O92` and `O93` (D157, D158). `O94` to `O96`
run as reopens and `O97` as a gap, each blocking a freeze; `O98` to `O101`
are notes.

- `OP-01/O94`: a reopen of OP-01/D158 against D26 and D99, on Local and SFTP
  (primed, reader A 1.b). D158: "A stream enters Exhausted when the read that
  delivers its last byte returns, the bytes delivered then reaching the
  length of the content fixed at open (D22)"; "A read on an Exhausted stream
  returns its end value (D132) with no backend contact, so it fails only
  with a caller error (D51)"; "Under E4 the end is the one a read meets
  (D26): the stream enters Exhausted when a read meets it". E4 "appends
  (file-system backends only)"; D26: "the read ends at the end it meets";
  D99: "POSIX `read(2)` returns 0 at the file's current end". Scenario: a
  stream on Local or SFTP over a 10-byte file; `read(10)` returns all ten
  bytes; O3 then appends two bytes, or does not; the next `read(10)`. The
  library cannot tell whether O3 acted. A CPython file object over Local
  returns the two appended bytes from that read ✓ run (3.13.18), and
  paramiko 5.1.0's `SFTPFile` knows no size and learns the end from a
  `CMD_READ` request ✓ read (`BufferedFile.read`, `SFTPFile._read`).
  A stream that reads on to meet the end contacts the backend after the
  last byte without E4, where on SFTP that request can fail (Failed) against
  "with no backend contact"; one that stops at the length fixed at open
  never delivers appended bytes under E4, against "when a read meets it".
  NP7 leaves "the content seen under O3's in-place modification, beyond
  D26's per-byte rule" unpromised, but D26's "the read ends at the end it
  meets" is that rule. To decide with the maintainer: on a file-system
  backend, whether a stream stops at the length fixed at open or reads on
  to meet the end, and what the read after the last byte may then surface.
  Dependent: D18, D22, D26, D96, D99, D158. Blocks the spec clause for the
  stream's entry into Exhausted on Local and SFTP. Decided by OP-01/D159.
- `OP-01/O95`: a reopen of OP-01/D158 against D41's NP2 (reader B 1a). D158:
  "A read on an Exhausted stream returns its end value (D132) with no backend
  contact"; NP2 leaves "The number of backend requests" unpromised for
  "every call and stream", and D41's option (a), as D107 restores it, ends
  "a test that asserts one is a defect"; D78 and D134: "the request count is
  NP2's". D41's check: "no row contradicts a decision". A check that
  asserts no request on an Exhausted read tests D158 and is a defect under
  D41. To decide with the maintainer: whether D158 promises zero requests,
  or only that such a read fails with a caller error alone (D51) and is
  Closed on a cancellation (D50), its request count NP2's. Dependent: D41,
  D78, D134, D158. Blocks the spec clause for a read on an Exhausted stream.
  Decided by OP-01/D160.
- `OP-01/O96`: a reopen of OP-01/D51 against D153 and `io` (reader A 1a and
  3a). D51's adopted option: "every failure but a caller error and a
  cancellation moves to Failed"; its reason: "any other failure may have
  consumed bytes". D153 gives `io.UnsupportedOperation` its own row, "an
  unsupported method (D70, D125) | `io.UnsupportedOperation` | builtin
  itself", not option (b)'s "counted as a caller error", so that "D33's
  caller errors stay apart from a method that never works". D51's Decision
  names a storage failure, a failure raised as is, a caller error and a
  cancellation only. Scenario: on an Open stream the caller calls `seek(0)`,
  catches `io.UnsupportedOperation`, then calls `read(3)`. By D51's option
  the stream is Failed and `read(3)` raises again (D52); D151's "as on an
  Open stream" and D125's "on a Failed one each answers as on an Open one"
  read the stream as usable. A CPython `io.BufferedReader` over a
  non-seekable raw stream returns `b'234'` from that `read(3)` after
  `read(2)` ✓ run (3.13.18), and D70 adopts `io`'s contract. To decide with
  the maintainer: the state after an unsupported method on an Open or
  Exhausted stream. Dependent: D18, D51, D70, D93 as D151 sets it, D125,
  D153. Blocks the spec clause for the unsupported methods' transition.
  Decided by OP-01/D161.
- `OP-01/O97`: a gap in OP-01/D152 for a cancelled read (reader B 2d).
  D152's D10 exception reaches "an open OP-01 read, whole or streamed, or a
  stream whose release a cancelled `close()` left pending (D118, D138)".
  D138's check reads D50's column, "a read or `close` is cancelled",
  as "releases; Closed", the release completing later under the deviation,
  and D20 has "a cancelled stream read releases the stream". So on an SFTP
  server running Windows (D83), a stream whose read was cancelled may be
  Closed while its release is pending, and an overlapping O2 replace may
  then fail, which D10 as D152 sets it does not admit. Not run (inferred
  from D138's `asyncio.to_thread` ✓ run in `OP-01/O76`). To decide with the
  maintainer: whether a cancelled read's release can be pending under
  D138's deviation, and whether D10's exception reaches it. Dependent: D10,
  D20, D50, D81, D138, D152. Blocks the spec clause for D10's exception
  under Overlapping mutation. Decided by OP-01/D162.
- `OP-01/O98`: a note on lists kept by rule that the close-out and the
  reopen extend (reader A 2a, 2b, 2c; reader B 2a, 2b, 2c). D104's O9 list,
  as D141 sets it, "D48, D53 to D56, D61, D62, D84, D86, D88, D99 and D124",
  lacks D153, which is headed AX-10 and whose Amends names D34 (D141's rule).
  D111 as D122 sets it, "D38's caller-level table, as D60, D61 and D98 amend
  it", lacks D153, which amends D38. D43's Decided-by cells under D120's
  rule, "every decision that changes what its normal value or a deviation
  lets a caller observe": Overlapping mutation "D65, D81, D83" and
  Cancelled close "D50, D94, D118, D138" lack D152; O3 in-place
  modification "D26, D48, D99" lacks D158's E4 sentence. To decide with the
  maintainer: each list. Blocks nothing here. Amended by OP-01/D163. Routed to OP-01's spec flow by OP-01/D163.
- `OP-01/O99`: a note on restatements later decisions leave behind (reader
  A 2d, 2e; reader B 1b, 1c, 1d, 3b, 3c). D80 as D92 sets it: "A valid call
  on a Closed stream raises `ValueError` (D15)", while D15 and D117 make
  `close()` a no-op, D137 has `writable()` "return false", and D154 has
  `write` raise `io.UnsupportedOperation`; D80's Amended-by names D92 and
  D93 only. D137's check: "Every other call on a Closed stream raises
  `ValueError` as before (D117)", against D154. D6's adopted option: "`b''`
  only at the end", against D157's `read(0)`. D70's check: "`read1` is the
  one method allowed fewer", while D7's chunks, `readline(limit)` and D134's
  `readinto1` return fewer too. D18's Open row, "next bytes (D6, D7); a
  failure moves to Failed", lacks D158's move to Exhausted, against D50's
  reason "one table holds every transition". D151 restates D125's
  `writable`, `isatty` and `flush` inside D93, against D150's reason "one
  list per method family". D155's option has `read1(n)` with n < 0 return
  "1 to r bytes", empty at r = 0, beside D78's "`b''` at the end", the value
  `io` gives (`read1()` at the end returns `b''` ✓ run). To decide with the
  maintainer: each text. Blocks nothing here. Amended by OP-01/D163. Routed to OP-01's spec flow by OP-01/D163.
- `OP-01/O100`: a note on D40's bound and `read1` with n < 0 (reader A 2f).
  D136: "D40's bound covers every read but those in NP9's scope"; D40: "plus
  what the caller's `read(n)` or chunk size asks for"; D155: "`read1(n)` with
  n < 0 returns 1 to r bytes", which asks for no size and may return the
  whole rest. `io.BufferedReader.read1()` over 10 MB returns 8192 bytes, one
  buffer ✓ run. Both hold if D40's bound caps D155's range. To decide with
  the maintainer: whether D40 or NP9 holds `read1(n)` with n < 0. Blocks
  nothing here. Amended by OP-01/D163. Routed to OP-01's spec flow by OP-01/D163.
- `OP-01/O101`: a note on D153's check (reader B 3a). D153: "other calls are
  plain and make no request, so no storage row and no cancellation reaches
  them". D20's scenario counts "`KeyboardInterrupt` in the sync form" as a
  cancellation, which needs no request, and D158 lets a cancellation reach
  an Exhausted read made "with no backend contact". To decide with the
  maintainer: the reason D153's cancellation cell gives. Blocks nothing here. Amended by OP-01/D163. Routed to OP-01's spec flow by OP-01/D163.

`OP-01/O102` to `O107` were found on 2026-11-04 by `/observe-review OP-01`
after the reopen that decided `O94` to `O97` (D159 to D162) and routed
`O98` to `O101` (D163). `O102` and `O103` are reopens and `O104` a gap,
each blocking a freeze; `O105` to `O107` are notes.

- `OP-01/O102`: a reopen of OP-01/D161 against D117, D125 and D154 (primed,
  reader A 1a and 4b, reader B 1a and 3a). D161: "in every state, an
  unsupported method (D70, D125, D153) raises `io.UnsupportedOperation`
  before any effect and leaves the state unchanged". D117's Closed rule, as
  D154 sets it: "every valid call but `close()`, `writable()` and `write()`
  raises `ValueError`"; D125: "On a Closed stream each raises `ValueError`
  (D117)", of `fileno()`, `writelines()` and `detach()`; D153's option (b):
  "a Closed stream's `seek` raises `ValueError`". D161's dialog asked "on an
  Open stream" and its option says "leaves every state as it was", naming
  no exception. Scenario: a stream is Closed; the caller calls `seek(0)`.
  D161 gives `io.UnsupportedOperation`, D117 `ValueError` (its base class,
  so `except ValueError` catches both, `except io.UnsupportedOperation` one
  only). A closed `io.BufferedReader` over a non-seekable raw stream raises
  `ValueError` from `seek`, `truncate`, `writelines` and `detach` and
  `io.UnsupportedOperation` from `write` and `fileno` ✓ run (3.13.18). To
  decide with the maintainer: what each unsupported method raises on a
  Closed stream. Dependent: D51, D70, D117, D125, D153, D154, D161. Blocks
  the spec clause for an unsupported method on a Closed stream. Decided by OP-01/D164.
- `OP-01/O103`: a reopen of OP-01/D159 against D160 and D41's NP2 (primed,
  reader A 1b, reader B 1b and 3b). D159's Decision: a stream "enters
  Exhausted when the bytes delivered reach the length of the content fixed
  at open (D22), and makes no backend contact after", its option "with no
  backend contact after". D160, from the same dialog: "The reason: it needs
  no backend contact. How many requests it makes is NP2's, as for every
  call"; NP2: "The number of backend requests", scope "every call and
  stream"; D41's option as D107 restores it: "a test that asserts one is a
  defect". D160 amends D158 only; its check says no contact "is the reason
  D159 gives as well, not a count", while D159's text states it as an
  outcome. Scenario: a check counts the backend requests after the last
  byte of a stream on Local and finds one; D159 makes that a failure, NP2 a
  defect in the check. The shape of `OP-01/O95`. To decide with the
  maintainer: whether D159's "makes no backend contact after" is a promise
  or D160's reason. Dependent: D41, D159, D160. Blocks the spec clause for
  the stream's entry into Exhausted. Decided by OP-01/D165.
- `OP-01/O104`: a gap in OP-01/D159 on Local (primed). D159: "On every
  backend a stream enters Exhausted when the bytes delivered reach the
  length of the content fixed at open (D22)"; its reason: "a length known
  at open bounds what a stream delivers"; D158: "it starts Exhausted for an
  empty item". On Linux, `/proc/self/status` is a regular file
  (`S_ISREG`) with `fstat` size 0 and 1417 bytes read, and
  `/sys/kernel/mm/transparent_hugepage/enabled` is one with size 4096 and
  23 bytes read ✓ run. Scenario: a Local store rooted where such a file is
  an item; O1 streams it, nothing changes it. With the size at open as the
  length, the first stream starts Exhausted and delivers no byte, and the
  second meets the end before the length without E4, a case D159 places
  under E4 only (a truncation, D99). No decision says how Local fixes the
  length at open, or what a stream does when the file does not hold it. To
  decide with the maintainer: the length a Local stream stops at for a file
  whose size at open is not its length, or that such files are out of
  OP-01's scope. Dependent: D6, D22, D158, D159. Blocks the spec clause for
  the stream's entry into Exhausted on Local. Decided by OP-01/D166.
- `OP-01/O105`: a note on texts D159 leaves behind (reader A 1c and 3a,
  reader B 1c). D158's adopted option: "under E4 the end is the one a read
  meets (D26)"; D26's dialog option: "the read ends at whatever end it
  meets"; D159: "the
  read ends at the earlier of the end it meets and the length fixed at
  open", amending both Decisions, not their options (as `OP-01/O99` records
  for D6's option and D157). D158's reason "matches D18's row labels, 'Open
  (bytes remain)'", while under D159 a stream on a truncated file is Open
  with no byte left until a read returns its end value; D159's check calls
  it consistent with D6's "the whole content once a read of nonzero size
  returns `b''`", which a truncation does not deliver (D26's non-promise and
  NP7 cover it). To decide with the maintainer: each text. Blocks nothing
  here. Amended by OP-01/D167. Routed to OP-01's spec flow by OP-01/D167.
- `OP-01/O106`: a note on D162's row (reader A 2a, 2b and 4a, reader B 2a).
  Its Decided-by cell, "D50, D94, D118, D138, D162", lacks D20 ("a cancelled
  stream read releases the stream") and D152, and Cancelled close's D49
  (`OP-01/O90`); D43's Overlapping mutation cell, "D65, D81, D83", lacks
  D162, whose D10 exception widens what that deviation lets O2 observe
  (D152 is `OP-01/O98`'s). D120's rule: "every decision that changes what
  its normal value or a deviation lets a caller observe". The row's normal
  value gives a cancelled stream read "the connection aborted (D118)",
  which D118 decided for `close()` and D162's option, widening "D138's
  deviation", does not state. To decide with the maintainer: each cell and
  the normal value's reach. Blocks nothing here. Amended by OP-01/D167. Routed to OP-01's spec flow by OP-01/D167.
- `OP-01/O107`: a note on RFC-0031 F2 (primed, reader A 4e). F2: "OP-01's
  freeze-2 reopen, already running when this RFC was accepted, re-witnesses
  each against the SDK or OS, or drops it", of `O29`, `O42`, `O76` and
  `O88`; the maintainer's answer in log `48d1bf11` (17:25Z), "One reopen
  for all", has the reopen re-witness them. The 2026-11-04 Session lines
  record walks of `O94` to `O101` only, and the four still cite 0.33:
  `_azure.py`, `_sftp.py`, `src/remote_store/aio/_sync_adapter.py`,
  `src/remote_store/backends/_memory.py`. Each item's outcome stands on its
  other evidence: O29 is decided by D73 from D9's and D41's text (Azure's
  `hdi_isfolder` is in no `azure.storage` package of the hatch env, Grep ✓;
  the service not run); O42 on paramiko's `rename` and `posix_rename` ✓;
  O76 on `asyncio.to_thread` ✓ run; O88 on `io.BytesIO` ✓ run. To decide
  with the maintainer: re-witness or drop each 0.33 citation. Blocks
  nothing here. Amended by OP-01/D167. Decided by OP-01/D167.

`OP-01/O108` was found on 2026-11-07 by the maintainer's review of PR #1208,
which carries the close-out D164 to D167. It is a gap, blocking a freeze.

- `OP-01/O108`: a gap in OP-01/D159 and D166 on SFTP. D159: "On every
  backend a stream enters Exhausted when the bytes delivered reach the
  length of the content fixed at open (D22)"; D166 fixes that length for
  Local only, and NP10's scope reads "Local streams"; D166's check: "an
  SFTP server's procfs or sysfs file reports a size at open the same way
  (not run), which D159 leaves to the size the server reports and NP10 does
  not reach". Scenario: an SFTP server on Linux serves `/proc/self/status`
  as an item; O1 streams it. With the reported size as the length the
  stream starts Exhausted (D158) and D6 promises a whole content it did not
  deliver; no decision says how SFTP fixes the length. To decide with the
  maintainer: the length an SFTP stream stops at for such a file. Dependent:
  D6, D22, D158, D159, D166. Blocks the spec clause for the stream's entry
  into Exhausted on SFTP. Decided by OP-01/D168.

`OP-01/O109` and `O110` were found on 2026-11-07 by the maintainer's review
of PR #1208 at `1d2f289`, after D168. `O109` is a gap and `O110` a reopen,
each blocking a freeze.

- `OP-01/O109`: a gap in OP-01/D168 on SFTP. D168: "On SFTP the length of
  the content fixed at open (D22, D159) is the size the server reports at
  open". SFTP attributes carry a size only when the size flag is set
  (draft-ietf-secsh-filexfer-02 § 5, not read here); paramiko 5.1.0's
  `SFTPAttributes` leaves `st_size` as `None` for an attributes message
  without `FLAG_SIZE` ✓ run. Scenario: an SFTP server reports an item's
  attributes without a size; O1 streams it. No decision fixes the length
  then, so D158's entry into Exhausted, D159's "the earlier of the end it
  meets and the length fixed at open" and NP10's "at most that size" have
  no length to work from. To decide with the maintainer: the length an
  SFTP stream stops at, and what is promised, when the server reports no
  size. Dependent: D6, D22, D158, D159, D166, D168. Blocks the spec clause
  for the stream's entry into Exhausted on SFTP. Decided by OP-01/D170.
- `OP-01/O110`: a reopen of OP-01/D168 against RFC-0029 R2 as RFC-0030 C3
  has it. D168's recommendation: "freeze-2 can follow the pull request
  that carries it"; its check: "A reopen by RFC-0030 C2". C3: "R2's full
  re-run is required after a reopen, not after a close-out"; R2: "A record
  is frozen only after `/observe-review` ... has re-run on it as its last
  reopen left it". D168 was also decided in a fix pass of the pull request,
  not in an `/observe` session (axes.md § Per-question loop), its ground
  read naming RFC-0030 and RFC-0031 but not the ground register. To decide
  with the maintainer, in an `/observe OP-01` session: D168's answer again
  under the session's ground read. Dependent: D159, D166, D168. Blocks the
  freeze; a full `/observe-review` follows that session. Decided by OP-01/D169.

`OP-01/O111` to `O117` were found on 2026-11-08 by `/observe-review OP-01`
after the reopen that decided `O109` and `O110` (D169, D170). `O111` to
`O113` are reopens and `O114` a gap, each blocking a freeze; `O115` to
`O117` are notes.

- `OP-01/O111`: a reopen of OP-01/D170 against D43's rule as D82 sets it
  (reader A 1b, reader B 1b). D82: "each variation is static per backend,
  never probed, never requested; a declaration may be conditioned on a
  stated property of the store's location (its file system, the server's
  platform)". D170's row: "unknown: a stream ends where a read meets the
  end, and the read that meets it can fail (an SFTP server that reports no
  size)"; its Decision applies "where the server reports no size at open",
  a fact of one item's attributes reply (paramiko 5.1.0 gives `st_size`
  `None` when the size flag is absent ✓ run). D170's check cites neither
  D43's rule nor D82. Scenario: an SFTP store whose server reports a size
  for some items and none for others. Read as a location property, SFTP
  declares the deviation for the store and a caller assumes the weaker
  value on every stream; read per reply, the row applies stream by stream,
  which D82's "static per backend" does not admit. To decide with the
  maintainer: what SFTP declares for Length at open, and how a caller
  learns which value a stream has. Dependent: D43, D44, D82, D170. Blocks
  the spec clause for D43's Length at open row and SFTP's profile row.
  Decided by OP-01/D171.
- `OP-01/O112`: a reopen of OP-01/D99 against D166 and D168 (reader A 1d).
  D99: "E12 reads: a transfer ends with fewer bytes than the backend
  announced for that transfer"; "On file-system backends a read that ends
  short of the item's size at open because of E4 is E4's". D166 and D168:
  "a stream ends at the earlier of the end a read meets and that size, with
  or without E4". D170's check: "with no size announced, a short end is
  never E12's", which reads a size reported at open as the announcement.
  Neither D166's nor D168's check cites D99. Scenario: a sysfs file, size
  4096 at open, 23 bytes read (✓ run, CPython 3.13.18), streamed on Local
  or through an SFTP server, no E4. D166 and D168 end the stream cleanly
  at 23 bytes (NP10); D99 exempts only a short end "because of E4", so the
  short end is E12's, which D28 surfaces as unavailable. To decide with the
  maintainer: whether a size at open is an announcement under E12, on
  Local and on SFTP. Dependent: D28, D99, D166, D168, D170. Blocks the spec
  clause for a stream's end on file-system backends. Decided by OP-01/D172.
- `OP-01/O113`: a reopen of OP-01/D155 against D70 (reader A 1a). D155's
  Decision, "(a), as stated", adopts: "`read1(n)` with n < 0 returns 1 to
  r bytes as soon as at least one is available ... none of these moves the
  position or the state". D70: "Each, like `read`, draws from the one
  position (D2)" and "`tell()` returns the bytes delivered so far". Read
  literally, `read1(-1)` delivers bytes and leaves `tell()` where it was,
  against D70 and D6's prefix. `io.BufferedReader.read1(-1)` over 10 bytes
  returns 10 and `tell()` is then 10 ✓ run (3.13.18). `OP-01/O99` holds a
  different text of D155's option (`read1` at r = 0). To decide with the
  maintainer: which of D155's cases "none of these" covers. Dependent: D6,
  D70, D78, D155. Blocks the spec clause for `read1` with n < 0. Decided by
  OP-01/D173.
- `OP-01/O114`: a gap in OP-01/O13 and D44 after D170 (reader A 2a, reader
  B 2a). `OP-01/O13`, as D138 and D162 set it, names D43's seven variation
  points, ending "Cancelled release"; D44: "OP-18 must expose D43's points:
  `OP-01/O13`". D170 adds an eighth, "Length at open", and its Amends,
  "OP-01/D18, OP-01/D43, OP-01/D158, OP-01/D159, OP-01/D168", do not name
  O13, where D76, D91, D138 and D162 each amended it on adding or renaming
  a point. OP-18's session lands seven points, so a caller cannot learn a
  store's Length at open. To decide with the maintainer: O13's list.
  Dependent: D43, D44, D170. Blocks the freeze: O13 is what OP-18's
  session reads. Decided by OP-01/D174.
- `OP-01/O115`: a note on texts D170 leaves behind (reader A 1c and 3a,
  reader B 1a and 3a). D169's recommendation, decided in D170's dialog:
  "one rule on every backend (D159)"; D159's: "it keeps D158's entry into
  Exhausted the same on every backend"; D158's: "it matches D18's row
  labels, 'Open (bytes remain)' ... and its 'starts Exhausted for an empty
  item'"; D168's: "it states for SFTP what D166 states for Local". D170:
  "D159's and D168's rule does not apply", and an empty item "starts
  Open", against D18's row label "Open (bytes remain)". D159's Decision and
  D168's adopted option: "no new variation point"; D170 adds one. D165's
  "The reason: no read after that needs the backend", which D170's check
  says "holds where a length is fixed at open only", unamended. To decide
  with the maintainer: each text. Blocks nothing here. Amended by
  OP-01/D175. Routed to OP-01's spec flow by OP-01/D175.
- `OP-01/O116`: a note on the register and D43's normal values (reader A
  2b, 2c, 2d, reader B 2b). D41's check: "no row contradicts a decision:
  each names the decision whose limit it marks", which D123 made true of
  NP8 and NP9; NP10, as D166 and D168 set it, names none ("Local and SFTP
  streams"). NP5's scope as D152 sets it, "D10, D118, D138", while D162
  widens NP5 to "after a cancellation", D20's cancelled read included.
  D43's Pinned resume normal value, "a dropped stream resumes pinned, or
  fails changed", while under E15 "a read that needs a new backend request
  fails not readable" (D47), D84 admits "not readable or changed" and D45
  a permission failure; its Decided-by cell lists D47 and D84. To decide
  with the maintainer: each text. Blocks nothing here. Amended by
  OP-01/D175. Routed to OP-01's spec flow by OP-01/D175.
- `OP-01/O117`: a note on `OP-01/O46` (reader B 2d). O46: "OP-01/D65
  declares that on Local under Windows an API replace, delete or move of an
  item may fail while an OP-01 read holds it open"; D83 adds "SFTP, where
  the server runs on Windows", D152 "a stream whose release a cancelled
  `close()` left pending" and D162 "a stream whose release a cancellation
  left pending". D65's Amended-by names D81 only, so the sessions O46
  addresses (OP-03, OP-04, OP-10, OP-12) reach none of the three from it.
  `OP-01/O56` was the same shape for O46's session list. To decide with
  the maintainer: O46's text. Blocks nothing here. Amended by OP-01/D175.
  Routed to OP-01's spec flow by OP-01/D175.
